def _arm_recorder():
    """R-E4-RECORD-LIVE + R-DAY1-BUNDLE A2: when the login lands, launch ALL FIVE recording lanes DETACHED
    (E4 chains, E7 ticks, E12 NSE/BSE, E13 depth, E14 positioning). Each self-gates on market hours + a
    fresh token (read-only quote/depth only, no orders). R-RESTART-RESUME: started through the one launcher —
    hidden, free RAM logged, never started twice. Then send exactly ONE line 'recorders: N/5 running' (this
    is the login's recorder line — no timer repeats it, A6). Best-effort; never fatal."""
    try:
        from scripts import launch_e1 as L
        procs = L.running_cmdlines()
        for label, args in L.RECORDERS:
            L.start(label, args, procs)                          # start any that are down; leaves the rest alone
        up, total, down = L.recorders_running()                  # re-read: how many are actually up now
        line = "[E1] recorders: %d/%d running" % (up, total)
        if down:
            line += " — down: " + ", ".join(down)
        notify(line)
    except Exception:
        pass


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

def test_start_stack_bat_launches_only_the_three():
    p = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), "Desktop", "E1-DESK", "START_E1_STACK.bat")
    if not os.path.exists(p):
        return
    launched = []
    for ln in open(p, encoding="utf-8"):
        s = ln.strip().lower()
        if s.startswith("start ") and "scripts\\" in s:
            launched.append(s.split("scripts\\", 1)[1].split(".py")[0])
    assert set(launched) == {"token_ping", "watchdog", "site_loop"}, launched   # never run_live

"""R-DAY1-BUNDLE A5: one card per fire per engine at fire time (PLACED/HELD), sent once per fire."""
from engine1_rebuild import book20_runner as BR
from scripts import portfolio_card as PC


def test_fire_card_format_has_tag_size_pattern_outcome():
    card = PC.fire_card("[E10]", "ABB", "direct", "when RSI is low → it goes up", 1300, "PAPER ORDER PLACED ₹1,300")
    assert "[E10]" in card and "ABB" in card and "BUY" in card and "₹1,300" in card
    assert "when RSI is low" in card and "PAPER ORDER PLACED ₹1,300" in card
    sell = PC.fire_card("[E1]", "TCS", "inverse", "p", 1000, "HELD — x")
    assert "SELL" in sell


def test_one_card_per_engine_once_per_fire(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    sent = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(BR, "FIRE_CARDS_SEEN", str(tmp_path / "seen.json"))
    import scripts.notify as N
    monkeypatch.setattr(N, "notify", lambda msg, *a, **k: sent.append(msg) or True)
    c = {"stock": 500209, "direction": "direct", "sentence": "when RSI is low → it goes up", "metric": "rsi"}
    # first fire → three cards, one per engine, tagged
    BR._fire_cards(c, "rsi", m_ok=False, placed=False, refire=False, cap_paused=False, guard_paused=False)
    assert len(sent) == 3 and any("[E1]" in m for m in sent) and any("[E10]" in m for m in sent) \
        and any("[E11]" in m for m in sent)
    assert all("HELD" in m for m in sent)          # held right now → HELD outcome, no PLACED
    # the SAME fire again in the day → no repeat (dedup)
    BR._fire_cards(c, "rsi", m_ok=False, placed=False, refire=False, cap_paused=False, guard_paused=False)
    assert len(sent) == 3, "a fire must card once per day per engine, never repeat"

    sent = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(BR, "FIRE_CARDS_SEEN", str(tmp_path / "seen2.json"))
    import scripts.notify as N
    monkeypatch.setattr(N, "notify", lambda msg, *a, **k: sent.append(msg) or True)

    BR._fire_cards(c, "unknown_family", m_ok=True, placed=True, refire=False, cap_paused=False, guard_paused=False)
    assert len(sent) == 3 and all("PAPER ORDER PLACED" in m for m in sent)   # placed → PLACED, cold-start ₹1,000
    assert all("₹1,000" in m for m in sent)

tool_result: def _fire_cards(c, family, m_ok, placed, refire, cap_paused, guard_paused):
    """R-DAY1-BUNDLE A5: one card per fire per ENGINE (E1/E10/E11) at fire time — this engine's own size and
    one outcome line (PLACED ₹… or HELD — <lock>). Sent ONCE per (day, stock, pattern, engine); never a
    repeat, never a demo (the notifier drops demo/test). Best-effort; never blocks the order path."""
    try:
        from scripts import portfolio_card as PC
        from scripts.notify import notify
        from engine1_rebuild import engine_book
        sym = _sym(c["stock"]); sentence = str(c.get("sentence", "") or "a certified same-stock pattern")
        if c.get("management"):                            # R-MORNING-22 item 1: survivor cards carry the management
            sentence = "%s — %s" % (sentence, c.get("management"))
        direction = c.get("direction", "direct")
        day = clock.iso()[:10]
        seen = _load_seen()
        outcome_held = _fire_lock_reason(m_ok, refire, cap_paused, guard_paused)
        for eng in ("E1", "E10", "E11"):
            key = "%s|%s|%s|%s" % (day, sym, sentence, eng)
            if key in seen:
                continue
            w = 1.0 if eng == "E1" else engine_book.weight(eng, family)
            risk = round(w * money.PAPER_R_RUPEES, 2)
            try:
                if placed:                                 # R-EVENING-22 B1: only PLACED (a real paper trade)
                    notify(PC.fire_card("[%s]" % eng, sym, direction, sentence, risk,
                                        "PAPER ORDER PLACED %s" % PC.R(risk)))   # reaches Telegram
                else:
                    _log_held(eng, sym, sentence, outcome_held, day)  # HELD: log + site 'held' list, never Telegram
                seen[key] = day
            except Exception:
                pass
        _save_seen(seen)
    except Exception:
        pass


def _load_seen():
    try:
        with open(FIRE_CARDS_SEEN, encoding="utf-8") as f:
            d = json.load(f)
        today = clock.iso()[:10]
        return {k: v for k, v in d.items() if str(v) == today}   # keep only today's -> the file self-prunes

tool_result: """Publish the site/ folder to the public GitHub Pages repo (owner ruling R-E1-SITE-PUBLISH).

Uses the GitHub CLI's existing auth on this laptop — no owner login, no secret written anywhere. Keeps
a working clone of `e1-live-site` beside the repo, mirrors site/ into it, and pushes only when
something changed. Called once after each site regeneration by site_loop.py. Static HTML + CSV only;
no secret is ever copied (the site folder contains none).
"""
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))

REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
SITE = os.path.join(REPO, "site")
PAGES_REMOTE = "https://github.com/[redacted]-[redacted]/e1-live-site.git"
PAGES_URL = "https://[redacted]-[redacted].github.io/e1-live-site/"
CLONE = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(REPO), "e1-live-site")      # sibling of the main repo
AUTHOR = ("use[redacted]ai-dev", "[redacted]")


def _git(args, cwd, timeout=180):
    from scripts import quiet
    return quiet.run(["git", *args], cwd=cwd, capture_output=True, text=True,
                     timeout=timeout, check=False)


def _ensure_clone():
    if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(CLONE, ".git")):
        return
    os.makedirs(CLONE, exist_ok=True)
    _git(["init", "-q", "-b", "main"], CLONE)
    _git(["remote", "add", "origin", PAGES_REMOTE], CLONE)


def _mirror():
    """Copy site/ into the clone (overwriting), keeping .git and .nojekyll. Returns nothing."""
    for name in os.listdir(CLONE):
        if name in (".git",):
            continue
        p = os.path.join(CLONE, name)
        shutil.rmtree(p) if os.path.isdir(p) else os.remove(p)
    for name in os.listdir(SITE):
        src, dst = os.path.join(SITE, name), os.path.join(CLONE, name)
        shutil.copytree(src, dst) if os.path.isdir(src) else shutil.copy2(src, dst)
    open(os.path.join(CLONE, ".nojekyll"), "w").close()         # serve files verbatim, no Jekyll
    # a 404 that lands on the live page keeps deep links working
    shutil.copy2(os.path.join(SITE, "index.html"), os.path.join(CLONE, "404.html"))


def publish(message="E1 live site refresh"):
    if not os.path.isdir(SITE):
        return {"published": False, "reason": "no site/ folder"}
    _ensure_clone()
    _mirror()
    if not _git(["status", "--porcelain"], CLONE).stdout.strip():
        return {"published": False, "reason": "unchanged", "url": PAGES_URL}
    _git(["add", "-A"], CLONE)
    _git(["-c", "user.name=%s" % AUTHOR[0], "-c", "user.email=%s" % AUTHOR[1],

def _foot():

    upd = live.get("published") or ""
    js = ("<script>function refreshTick(){fetch('../data/live.json?_='+Date.now()).then(function(r){return r.json()})"
          ".then(function(d){var t=(d.tape&&d.tape.stocks)||[];if(!t.length)return;var h=t.slice(0,40).map(function(s){"
          "var c=(s.change_pct||0)>=0?'gain':'loss';var ch=s.change_pct!=null?((s.change_pct>=0?'+':'')+s.change_pct+'%'):'';"
          "return \"<span>\"+s.stock+\" <b class='\"+c+\"'>\"+s.last+\"</b> \"+ch+\"</span>\";}).join('');"
          "var el=document.querySelector('.tick');if(el&&h)el.innerHTML=h;}).catch(function(){});}"
          "setInterval(refreshTick,120000);"
          "function tab(g,i){var ps=document.querySelectorAll('[data-g=\"'+g+'\"]');ps.forEach(function(p,j){"
          "p.classList.toggle('on',j==i);});var bs=document.querySelectorAll('[data-tb=\"'+g+'\"]');"
          "bs.forEach(function(b,j){b.classList.toggle('on',j==i);});}"
          "function filt(id,tid){var q=(document.getElementById(id).value||'').toUpperCase();"
          "document.querySelectorAll('#'+tid+' tbody tr').forEach(function(r){"
          "r.style.display=r.getAttribute('data-k').indexOf(q)>=0?'':'none';});}</script>")
    return ("<p class='updated'>last updated " + esc(upd) + " · the tape refreshes every 2 minutes</p>"
            + js + "</body></html>")

=== build() ===
def build(publish_gate=True):
    """Generate every page into a STAGING dir, run the build gate, and only swap the pages into site/v2/ when
    the gate is clean. On failure it raises RenderGateError and leaves the last-good site/v2/ untouched — so a
    broken build never publishes (R-V2-FIX item 4)."""
    global _OUT
    import shutil
    import tempfile
    _write_diaries_data()                                  # data files (shared) — safe to refresh either way
    staging = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="v2stage_")
    saved = _OUT
    _OUT = staging
    try:
        _leaderboard(); _engines(); _stocks(); _patterns(); _deliverables(); _data_page(); _results(); _how(); _ask()
        _scoreboard()
        problems = render_gate(staging) if publish_gate else []
        jargon = no_jargon_ok(staging) if publish_gate else []
        if problems or jargon:
            raise RenderGateError("v2 build gate FAILED (site/v2/ left untouched): %s%s" % (
                problems, (" | jargon: %s" % jargon) if jargon else ""))
        # gate clean -> swap staging into V2 (remove old pages first so a renamed page can't linger)
        os.makedirs(V2, exist_ok=True)
        for stale in glob.glob(os.path.join(V2, "*.html")):
            try:
                os.remove(stale)
            except OSError:
                pass
        for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(staging, "*.html")):
            shutil.copy2(p, os.path.join(V2, os.path.basename(p)))
    finally:
        _OUT = saved
        shutil.rmtree(staging, ignore_errors=True)
    return {"out": V2, "pages": len(_pages_on_disk(V2))}


def _demo():
    build()
    problems = render_gate()
    assert not problems, problems
    assert not no_jargon_ok()
    # the certified count is sane (not the minute-validation tally) and appears on the patterns page
    n = certified_count()
    assert 0 <= n < 100000, n
    pat = open(os.path.join(V2, "patterns.html"), encoding="utf-8").read()
    assert ("%d patterns passed" % n) in pat and "[object Object]" not in pat
    # holds-on renders names, not objects
    assert "Holds on:" in pat
    # ticker is resolved, never a spinner
    idx = open(os.path.join(V2, "index.html"), encoding="utf-8").read()
    assert "loading" not in idx.lower()
    print("site_v2 demo OK: %d pages server-rendered, gate clean, count=%d, no jargon, no spinner." % (
        len(_pages_on_disk()), n))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    _demo() if "--demo" in sys.argv else print(build())

def _old_page_redirects():
    for old, tab in OLD_PAGES.items():
        with open(os.path.join(SITE, old), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(f'<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><meta http-equiv="refresh" content="0; url=index.html#{tab}">'
                    f'<title>Moved</title><a href="index.html#{tab}">This page moved to the E1 Terminal.</a>')


def build(snapshot=None):
    """FULL cycle (SITE-CYCLE split, ~every 10 min): the live data (via build_live) PLUS every page and the
    heavy screener/patterns snapshot. R-E1-TERMINAL: the site is the static terminal in terminal/ plus the JSON
    it polls; the page functions above are the retired first site, kept for reference and no longer called."""
    import time
    from scripts import terminal_snapshot
    build_live()                                        # the live data first, so a full cycle is also current
    # the heavy screener/patterns snapshot refreshes about hourly (SNAPSHOT_MAX_AGE_S), never every cycle.
    scr = os.path.join(SITE, "data", "screener.json")
    stale = not os.path.exists(scr) or time.time() - os.path.getmtime(scr) > SNAPSHOT_MAX_AGE_S
    if snapshot is None:
        try:
            from scripts.memory_guard import should_throttle
            snapshot = stale and not should_throttle()
        except Exception:
            snapshot = stale
    if snapshot:
        terminal_snapshot.build()
    # R-E1-FACTS-PAGE: refresh the daily 323-facts page (site/facts.html + reports/FACTS-PAGE.md)
    try:
        from scripts import facts_page
        facts_page.build()
    except Exception:
        pass
    # R-DATA-LANES: refresh the data-readiness dashboard (site/data-readiness.html + reports/DATA-READINESS.md)
    try:
        from scripts import data_lanes, graveyard, allocator, roof_scoreboard, engines_page, results_page
        data_lanes.build(); graveyard.build(); allocator.build(); roof_scoreboard.build()
        engines_page.build(); results_page.build()     # R-RESULTS-PAGE: results.html + per-test blotters
        from scripts import deliverables_page, messages_page, recording_page
        deliverables_page.build()                       # R-EVENING-NOW (1): site/deliverables.xlsx (all calls)
        messages_page.build()                           # R-EVENING-NOW amend 3: site/data/messages.json (30d)
        recording_page.build()                          # R-EVENING-NOW amend 4: site/data/recording.json (lanes)
        from scripts import engine_page
        engine_page.build()                             # R-DEEP-DIVE: site/engine-e1.html (deep dive)
        from scripts import site_v2
        site_v2.build()                                # R-NIGHT-2 Part B: site/v2/ preview
        try:                                           # R-MORNING-22 item 4: capture today's E21 15:25/09:20
            from engine21_overnight import overnight as _E21   # marks through the day (idempotent, read-only)
            _E21.record_marks()
        except Exception:
            pass

tool_result: def publish(message="E1 live site refresh"):
    if not os.path.isdir(SITE):
        return {"published": False, "reason": "no site/ folder"}
    _ensure_clone()
    _mirror()
    if not _git(["status", "--porcelain"], CLONE).stdout.strip():
        return {"published": False, "reason": "unchanged", "url": PAGES_URL}
    _git(["add", "-A"], CLONE)
    _git(["-c", "user.name=%s" % AUTHOR[0], "-c", "user.email=%s" % AUTHOR[1],
          "commit", "-q", "-m", message + "\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 <[redacted]>"],
         CLONE)
    push = _git(["push", "-q", "-u", "origin", "main"], CLONE)
    return {"published": push.returncode == 0, "url": PAGES_URL,
            "error": (push.stderr or "").strip()[:200] if push.returncode else None}


def enable_pages():
    """Turn on GitHub Pages from main/root via the API (idempotent)."""
    r = quiet.run(["gh", "api", "-X", "POST", "repos/[redacted]-[redacted]/e1-live-site/pages",
                        "-f", "source[branch]=main", "-f", "source[path]=/"],
                       capture_output=True, text=True, check=False)
    ok = r.returncode == 0 or "already exists" in (r.stderr + r.stdout).lower()
    return {"pages_enabled": ok, "detail": (r.stderr or r.stdout).strip()[:200]}


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("publish:", publish("E1 live site — publish"))
    if "--enable" in sys.argv:
        print("pages:", enable_pages())

def _write(name, body):
    os.makedirs(_OUT, exist_ok=True)
    with open(os.path.join(_OUT, name), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(body)


# ---------- pages ----------
def esc(x):
    if x is None:
        return "—"
    if isinstance(x, float) and x != x:          # NaN -> em-dash, never the literal 'nan'
def _data(name):
    try:
        return json.load(open(os.path.join(DATA, name), encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        return {}

def _scoreboard():
    """R-ANALYST-SCOREBOARD: the analyst scoreboard page in the v2 style, shelf-only. Server-rendered from
    reports/ANALYST-SCOREBOARD.json — honest about what was captured and what is blocked; never a guessed call."""
    try:
        d = json.load(open(os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "ANALYST-SCOREBOARD.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        d = {}
    sources = d.get("sources") or []
    srows = ""
    for s in sources:
        state = s.get("state") or ""
        cls = "gain" if state == "REACHABLE" else "warn"
        srows += "<tr><td>%s</td><td class='sub'>%s</td><td class='%s'>%s</td><td class='sub'>%s</td></tr>" % (
            esc(s.get("source")), esc(s.get("kind")), cls, esc(state), esc(s.get("reason")))
    ranking = d.get("ranking") or []
    rrows = ""
    for r in ranking[:50]:
        rrows += ("<tr><td>%s</td><td>%s</td><td class='n'>%s</td><td class='n'>%s</td><td class='n'>%s</td>"
                  "<td class='n'>%s</td></tr>" % (
                      esc(r.get("analyst")), esc(r.get("stock")), esc(r.get("horizon")), esc(r.get("n")),
                      pct(r.get("accuracy_pct")), pct(r.get("recent_accuracy_pct"))))
    rank_tbl = ("<table><thead><tr><th>Analyst</th><th>Stock</th><th>Days held</th><th>Calls</th>"
                "<th>Right (lifetime)</th><th>Right (90 days)</th></tr></thead><tbody>" + rrows + "</tbody></table>"
                ) if rrows else ("<div class='panel sub'>No calls captured yet — the sources below are robots-gated "
                                 "or paywalled, and no analyst call is ever guessed.</div>")

tool_use Bash {"command": "sed -n '155,216p' scripts/exams_real.py", "description": "Read exams_real.run"}
tool_result: def run(write=True, limit=None):
    cost_rt, measured = calibrated_cost_rt()
    k, k_meas = LB.impact_k()
    cal = LB.spread_calibration()
    cand = evening.active_daily_direction_signals()
    reader = lambda t: ds.read(int(t), "raw")
    reexamined = certified = 0
    certs = []
    probation = []
    if not len(cand):
        return {"note": "no candidates on disk", "reexamined": 0, "certified": 0}
    rows = cand.sort_values("hold_margin", ascending=False)
    if limit:
        rows = rows.head(limit)
    for _, r in rows.iterrows():
        tok = int(r["stock"]); cond = r["condition"]; metric = r["metric"]
        if not isinstance(cond, str):
            continue
        try:
            daily = TF.glue(reader(tok), "daily")
        except Exception:
            continue
        n = len(daily)
        if n < RVOL_WIN + 40:
            continue
        mask = evening._window_mask(daily, metric, cond)
        if mask is None:
            continue
        positions = list(np.flatnonzero(mask))
        direction = r["direction"]
        fam = "cross-metric" if "+" in str(metric) else (str(metric).split("[")[0].strip() or "other")
        reexamined += 1
        best = certify_candidate(tok, positions, daily, direction, fam, cost_rt, k, k_meas, cal)
        if best:
            certified += 1
            best.update({"stock": LB._sym(tok), "family": fam})
            certs.append(best)
        else:
            probation.append({"stock": LB._sym(tok), "family": fam,
                              "reason": "no management variant survived real costs + the luck correction"})
    # trades/day of the certified set = sum of fires across the kept variants over the walk-forward span
    total_fires = sum(c["fires"] for c in certs)
    days = _walk_days(cand, reader)
    tpd = round(total_fires / max(1, days), 2)
    out = {
        "note": "STAGING ONLY — the live book and the live exam are unchanged; switching is a separate ruling.",
        "cost": {"calibrated_round_trip_bps": round(cost_rt * 10000, 1), "measured": measured},
        "grid": {"horizons": list(HORIZONS), "stops_atr": list(STOPS), "trail": list(TRAIL),
                 "variants_per_candidate": V, "luck_z": round(_Z_LUCK, 3),
                 "luck_note": "Wilson lower bound at a Bonferroni z across all %d variants tried" % V},
        "reexamined": reexamined, "certified": certified, "probation": len(probation),
        "certified_trades_per_day": tpd, "walk_forward_days": days,
        "certified_patterns": certs, "probation_patterns": probation[:200],
    }
    if write:
        with open(STAGING_JSON, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(out, f, indent=1, default=str)
        _write_md(out)
    return out
